【Fed Chair Kevin Warsh: Committed to fulfilling both inf and employment mandates】Fed Chair Kevin Warsh told the House Financial Services Committee he places equal weight on the Fed’s employment and inf goals, reiterating he has emphasized the latter since taking office. He said the Fed does not favor one part of its mandate over the other and that price stability and full employment are not mutually exclusive; he is committed to achieving both. When asked about targeted measures to narrow Black-white unemployment and opportunity gaps, Warsh said the U.S. must not leave anyone behind and that broad economic opportunity is critical to growth over the next five to 10 years, meaning every American needs the chance to be productive.

### 2026-07-15 00:41:33  #焦點 #國際

【E·吉恩·卡羅爾收到特朗普支付的560萬美元民事案件賠償金】美國聯邦法院卷宗記錄顯示，特朗普已向作家E·吉恩·卡羅爾付清560萬美元賠償金。陪審團認定，20世紀90年代特朗普在曼哈頓一家百貨商店內對卡羅爾實施性侵，後續又公開誹謗她，據此裁定特朗普承擔賠償責任。2023年陪審團作出賠付裁決後，特朗普提起上訴，這筆賠償金此前一直交由第三方託管。案件線上庭審記錄於本週二新增備註顯示，託管款項已於上週撥付至卡羅爾賬戶。此次付款的前提是美國最高法院駁回了特朗普針對該判決的最後一次上訴申請，審理該案的法官隨即下令將託管賠償金髮放給卡羅爾。卡羅爾的代理律師羅伯塔·卡普蘭發表宣告表示，她與當事人“十分欣慰地告知公眾，當事人已收到陪審團依據判決裁定的損害賠償金”。

### 2026-07-15 00:28:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Italy #Stock Market #Importance 0

FTSE MIB Closes Slightly Higher — The FTSE MIB rose 0.1% to close at 52,862 on Tuesday, supported by lower-than-expected inflation data in the US. The data eased expectations of a July Fed rate hike, although financial stocks posted mixed performances. Fincantieri jumped 2.7% after unveiling plans to integrate artificial intelligence into its technological innovation strategy, while Intesa Sanpaolo (0.3%) reaffirmed its buy rating on the stock. Utilities also advanced amid increased cooling demand, led by A2A (1.8%). Hera gained 1.2% after signing a binding agreement to acquire a 70% stake in Kronos Polymer Polska, while oil major Eni added 0.7%, supported by volatile oil prices. Inwit was the session's worst performer, falling 3.8% after an Italian court rejected its request to suspend the Fastweb+Vodafone termination from the MSA. Meanwhile, the European Central Bank selected banks and payment firms for the digital euro project, including BPMS (0.7%), Nexi (0.2%) and UniCredit (-0.5%).

US consumer prices declined in June for the first time in six years, while core inflation was little changed, easing pressure on the Federal Reserve to raise interest rates. Financials traded higher, with BNP Paribas up 1.4% and Credit Agricole adding 1.6%. Meanwhile, TotalEnergies advanced 1.1% as oil prices rose amid ongoing tensions in the Middle East. Other notable gainers included Schneider Electric (+1.7%) and Engie (+0.9%). In contrast, luxury stocks tumbled on rising geopolitical risks. LVMH lost 1.7%, L'Oreal shed 0.8%, and Kering retreated 2.4%. Safran (-0.7%), Sanofi (-2.1%), and EssilorLuxottica (-2.5%) also posted losses.

European Stocks Inch Higher — European stocks rebounded to close slightly higher on Tuesday after soft inflation data in the US reduced sovereign yields in major economies. The Euro STOXX 50 inched higher by 0.2% to 6,283 and the STOXX Europe 600 rose 0.1% to 642. Both headline and core inflation rates in the US declined more than expected in June, shutting the door on a Fed hike this month and adding some respite for bonds across major economies, improving credit conditions for European corporate giants. Banks benefited from the data, with BNP Paribas, ING, and Deutsche Bank adding more than 1%. Power operators also rose sharply amid the rebound in AI stocks, tracking companies with exposure to AI infrastructure. Schneider added 1.7% and Siemens Energy advanced 3%. Meanwhile, ASML rose by 0.7% ahead of its earnings tomorrow.

FTSE 100 Edges Up on Tuesday — The FTSE 100 edged slightly higher on Tuesday in a volatile session, supported by softer US inflation data and President Donald Trump’s decision to drop plans for a fee on cargo passing through the Strait of Hormuz. US annual inflation slowed to 3.5% in June from 4.2% in May, below expectations of 3.8%, prompting investors to reduce bets on further Federal Reserve tightening. Market sentiment also improved after Trump replaced his proposed 20% transit fee with investment agreements from Gulf states, saying the revenue would instead come through future US investments. Energy and financial stocks led gains, with Shell up 0.9% and BP adding 1.6%. HSBC rose 1.7%, Lloyds Banking gained 1%, and Standard Chartered advanced 1.6%. Mining stocks also performed strongly, with Rio Tinto up 3% and Glencore rising 3.2%. On the downside, AstraZeneca fell more than 1.5%, GSK declined nearly 2%, while Unilever and Rolls Royce slipped around 0.6%.

### 2026-07-14 23:11:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Holds Above $83 — Brent crude oil steadied above $83 per barrel on Tuesday after gaining more than 4% earlier in the session and following a 9.6% surge the previous day, as markets assessed renewed Middle East supply risks. The collapse of the US-Iran truce and fresh attacks on shipping through the Strait of Hormuz increased concerns over disruptions at the key energy chokepoint. The United Arab Emirates reported that two oil tankers were struck while using the southern shipping lane, highlighting risks to one of the region’s main export routes. Tracking vessel movements has become more difficult as some ships operate without active transponders. The US also announced plans to resume a blockade on Iranian oil exports and potentially charge vessels using Hormuz under American protection. Despite the tensions, Iran continued moving oil through the strait, while the UAE boosted production to 3.8 million barrels per day in June after increasing exports through alternative shipping arrangements.

The United Arab Emirates said two of its tankers were struck while transiting the waterway, highlighting growing risks to one of the world's busiest energy routes. Ecuador Inflation Rises in June — Ecuador's annual inflation rate rose to 1.65% in June 2026 from 0.92% in the previous month. The strongest price pressures were recorded in transport (7.8%) and healthcare (3.6%). Prices also increased for education (1.6%), recreation and culture (1.2%), communication (0.7%), and furniture and household articles (1.0%). Meanwhile, deflation persisted in food and non-alcoholic beverages (-0.2%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (-0.9%), clothing and footwear (-4.3%), and housing and utilities (-2.7%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.8% after falling 0.6% in May.

### 2026-07-14 22:57:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Trade Higher — US stocks moved higher on Tuesday after softer inflation data shut the door on a Fed rate hike this month. The S&P 500 and Nasdaq 100 rose up to 0.5%, while the Dow swung to hover above the flatline. Both headline and core inflation rates declined, easing the impact of another rally in energy prices after the US and Iran maintained strikes against each other. Chip producers rebounded from yesterday's selloff with Micron and Sandisk adding 4% each, tracking major memory producers, while Nvidia rose 1%. On top of that, financial companies rebounded from early losses. JPMorgan jumped 3% after posting its strongest profit level ever, while Bank of America, and Citi gained over 2% after releasing their results. Among capital markets, Goldman Sachs surged over 7% after beating earnings estimates. On the other hand, IBM tanked 25% after missing expectations and warning of low spending from clients, which have opted for spending on chips and memory this quarter instead.

### 2026-07-14 22:54:25  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #XAUUSD #Impact bearish #EURUSD

【Kevin Warsh reiterates commitment to 2% disinflation target, stresses Fed independence】Kevin Warsh told a congressional hearing he will not provide forward guidance on interest rates and largely avoided discussing specific rate policy. He said the Fed is firmly committed to price stability and to driving disinflation back to a 2% target, and that the central bank has the tools to do so. Warsh said the Fed will reassess its inflation framework to better understand drivers of inflation and potential policy responses. Newly created working groups remain in a research phase; discussions will occur among the 19 policymakers, with the process conducted transparently and research and policy ideas shared periodically. On balance-sheet policy he said the balance sheet is part of monetary policy, not just market plumbing, and any adjustments would be communicated well in advance to give markets time to prepare. Asked about potential political interference, including from Trump, Warsh said he will continue to execute his duties and defend the Fed’s policy independence.

TSX Rises on Softer US Inflation Data — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose 0.5% to trade near 35,500 on Tuesday following the release of US inflation data. US consumer prices declined in June for the first time in six years, while core inflation was little changed, easing pressure on the Federal Reserve to raise interest rates. Financials traded higher, with RBC, TD Bank, BMO, and CIBC each gaining nearly 1%. Meanwhile, the BoC is widely expected to keep its key policy rate unchanged at 2.25% on Wednesday. In contrast, oil prices rose on renewed concerns over crude shipments through the Strait of Hormuz amid the escalating US-Iran conflict, fueling worries about higher energy costs. Gold prices also advanced, supporting mining stocks. Agnico Eagle, Franco-Nevada, and WPM gained more than 2%, while Barrick added over 2.5%.

Baltic Dry Index Up for 4th Day — The Baltic Exchange's dry bulk freight index, which monitors rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, was up for the fourth session on Tuesday, rising about 0.7% to a new high since June 5 at 2,980 points, driven by gains across all vessel segments. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, advanced for the fourth day, increasing by 1% to its highest in over a month at 4,751 points; and the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, edged up 0.1% to 2,251 points. Among smaller vessels, the supramax index increased by 0.2% to a peak since June 2022 at 1,710 points.

### 2026-07-14 21:38:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Bund Yields Ease Slightly After US Inflation Data — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield trimmed its rise to 3.09% after softer-than-expected US inflation data for June reduced expectations for Federal Reserve rate hikes this year. However, yields remained at their highest level since May 21, as Middle East tensions raised concerns that higher energy prices could fuel inflation and push interest rates higher. The two-year bond yield, more sensitive to policy rate expectations, also climbed to 2.8%, its highest since July 2024. The US military continued strikes against Iran after President Donald Trump reinstated a blockade on Iranian shipping and proposed a 20% fee to guard the Strait of Hormuz, increasing uncertainty over energy flows. Markets responded by betting on further European Central Bank rate hikes, with money markets pricing in a deposit rate of 2.70% by December (up from the current 2.25%) and fully expecting a September hike.

US Stocks Inch Higher After Soft CPI — US equity indices were mostly higher on Tuesday after softer inflation data shut the door on a Federal Reserve rate hike this month. The S&P 500 inched higher and the Nasdaq 100 rose 0.7%, while the Dow erased a sharp deficit to hover only slightly under the flatline. Both headline and core inflation rates declined, easing the impact of another rally in energy prices after the US and Iran maintained strikes against each other. Chip producers rebounded from yesterday's selloff with Micron and Sandisk adding 5% each, tracking major memory producers, while Nvidia rose 1%. On the other hand, the country's largest banks were lower. JPMorgan fell 2.5% despite posting its strongest profit level ever, and Bank of America dropped 1% despite beating earnings. Likewise, Wells Fargo sank 2% even though its earnings report was also seen as a beat. In turn, Goldman Sachs surged 4% on strong results for the period.

Euro Rebounds as Dollar Weakens on Soft US Inflation — The euro climbed to $1.145, its strongest level since June 19, supported by broad dollar weakness after softer-than-expected US inflation data eased pressure on the Federal Reserve to raise rates this year. The US military continued strikes against Iran after President Donald Trump reinstated a blockade on Iranian shipping and proposed a 20% fee to guard the Strait of Hormuz, heightening uncertainty over energy flows. 【Vedanta Oil & Gas to invest $5 bln over five years to lift output to ~500,000 boe/d】Vedanta Oil & Gas said it will invest $5 billion over the next five years to raise oil and gas output to about 500,000 barrels of oil equivalent per day, more than fivefold its recent level. The company reported average production of 87,200 bbl/d in the fiscal year ended March 31 (down from over 210,000 bbl/d a decade ago) and expects about 100,000 bbl/d this fiscal year. Vedanta said the investment will focus on improved recovery at mature fields and new field development to offset declining mature-field output and help reduce India’s energy import reliance; it aims for its volumes to account for over half of India’s crude production.

UK Gilt Yields Ease but Remain Close to Two-Month Highs — UK 10-year gilt yields fell back below 5%, though they remained near two-month highs, tracking a decline in US Treasury yields after softer-than-expected US inflation data reduced expectations for Federal Reserve rate hikes this year. However, Middle East tensions raised concerns that rising energy prices could reignite inflation and push interest rates higher, leading markets to increase bets on Bank of England rate hikes. Investors now nearly fully expect two increases in 2026, with a September hike fully priced in. Attention also shifted to UK politics, as Andy Burnham prepares to replace Keir Starmer as prime minister on July 20. The focus is on his choice for finance minister, with Ed Miliband, seen as fiscally expansive, emerging as the favorite. This prospect has unsettled gilt investors, who are already wary of Britain’s fiscal fragility.

【Palantir CEO批評AI行業高管：一邊宣揚失業風險 一邊賺得盆滿缽滿】Palantir執行長亞歷克斯・卡普在採訪中表示，AI固然會整體提升民眾生活水平，但行業頂層群體攫取的收益，和其他領域的收入將形成極度失衡的懸殊差距。卡普在節目中談道：“普通民眾的生活質量會有所改善，但身處AI核心圈層的從業者，財富規模或將在原有基礎上翻十倍甚至上百倍。” 卡普認為，即便AI不會造成大規模崗位流失，大眾依舊會為此深感焦慮。不少技術領域從業者早已把這類行業收縮、崗位替代描述成無可避免的必然結果。 “手握實驗室企業管理權的管理層，一直在向外界灌輸這套說辭。他們直白告訴大家，普通人未來的生活會變得糟糕不堪，而他們自己卻藉此賺得盆滿缽滿，這也是這群人很難獲得大眾好感的原因。”卡普並未點名具體高管，但AI企業Anthropic的達里奧・阿莫代伊、OpenAI的山姆・奧特曼，此前都曾警示AI或將引發失業潮，只是後續二人相關表態有所軟化。

### 2026-07-14 21:15:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Currency #Importance 1

Pound Rises on UK Political Shift, Rate Hikes Bets — The pound rose to $1.34, returning to mid-June levels, as investors turned their attention to UK politics. Andy Burnham, set to replace Keir Starmer as prime minister on July 20, and his choice of finance minister are now in the spotlight. Betting markets currently favor Ed Miliband, widely viewed as fiscally expansive, for the role. Investors also weighed escalating Middle East tensions, rising oil prices, and inflation concerns. The US military continued strikes against Iran after President Donald Trump reinstated a blockade on Iranian shipping and proposed a 20% fee to guard the Strait of Hormuz, heightening uncertainty over energy flows. Markets responded by pricing in further Bank of England rate hikes, nearly fully expecting two increases in 2026, with a September hike now fully priced in. In the US, softer June inflation data eased Fed rate hike pressure, though Governor Christopher Waller warned of possible action if inflation stays above 2%.

Warsh Reaffirms Fed's Commitment to Price Stability — Fed Chair Warsh said policymakers remain fully committed to restoring price stability and have no tolerance for persistently elevated inflation, according to prepared remarks released ahead of his Semiannual Monetary Policy Report to Congress. Warsh added, "If we get policy right - and we will - the inflation surge of the last five years will be a thing of the past". The Fed Chair also said the US economy continues to expand at a solid pace, demonstrating resilience despite recent developments. Household consumption is growing at a moderate rate, while manufacturing output has risen steadily this year. He highlighted business investment as the economy's most notable strength, driven largely by the construction of data centers and robust demand for AI-related equipment and software. On the labor market, Warsh noted that job creation has kept pace with labor force growth, the unemployment rate remains low while nominal wages continue to post solid growth.

### 2026-07-14 21:06:05  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Kpler: Russian refining hits 21-year low after drone strikes】Kpler said Ukrainian drone attacks have reduced Russian refinery throughput to a 21-year low of about 3.8 mln bpd. The firm expects Q3 refinery runs to remain subdued, with only limited recovery as ongoing strikes and repair work constrain operations. Cumulative damage has sharply raised offline capacity: as of mid‑July roughly 4.3 mln bpd of capacity was offline or affected, about 58% of Russia’s refining capacity, and an estimated 1.5–2.0 mln bpd of processing capability is fully out of operation. Kpler forecasts a modest rebound to around 4.3 mln bpd in August as maintenance eases, but says continued drone attacks pose significant downside risk.

【歐盟與英國正式簽署有關直布羅陀的條約】當地時間7月14日，歐盟與英國在布魯塞爾正式簽署關於直布羅陀的條約。根據條約，直布羅陀居民可憑居留卡進入西班牙，西班牙公民進入直布羅陀時可使用政府身份證件。抵達直布羅陀機場的旅客將接受直布羅陀和西班牙邊境官員的護照檢查。條約由歐盟貿易委員、英國歐洲事務國務大臣、西班牙外交大臣以及直布羅陀首席部長多方共同簽署。該條約基於2025年達成的協議。直布羅陀位於伊比利亞半島南端，是英國海外領地，與西班牙接壤。自2016年英國透過脫離歐盟的公投以來，英國控制下的直布羅陀與歐盟之間的邊界始終是雙方未達成共識的問題。（央視新聞）

【科技部部長陰和俊會見沙特通訊和資訊科技大臣阿卜杜拉·蘇瓦哈】7月13日，科技部部長陰和俊在北京會見了沙特通訊和資訊科技大臣阿卜杜拉·蘇瓦哈。雙方就深化中沙科技創新合作深入交換意見並達成共識。陰和俊表示，中沙關係源遠流長，科技合作前景廣闊。近年來，在兩國元首戰略引領下，雙方在聯合研究、科技人文交流、創新創業等方面開展了卓有成效的合作。中方願同沙方一道，積極落實兩國元首重要共識，進一步發揮中沙政府間科技合作聯委會等機制作用，不斷拓展中沙科技合作的深度和廣度，推動兩國科技創新合作取得新進展。蘇瓦哈表示，沙特始終視中國為重要的合作伙伴，願同中方一道，加強沙特“2030願景”和共建“一帶一路”倡議深入對接，拓展相關科技領域合作，不斷為中沙全面戰略伙伴關係注入新動力，推動兩國經濟社會繁榮進步，共同造福兩國人民。

Silver Surges as US Inflation Cools, Fed Rate Hike Bets Ease — Silver rose nearly 2% to $58.9 an ounce on Tuesday after weaker-than-expected US inflation data dampened expectations for Federal Reserve rate hikes. The headline CPI fell to 3.5% in June, driven by slowing energy inflation, while the core rate unexpectedly eased to 2.6%, both below forecasts. Month-over-month, the CPI dropped 0.4%, its first decline since 2020. Still, annual rates remain well above the Fed’s target. Investors now await Federal Reserve Chair Kevin Warsh’s first congressional testimony for signals on a potential September hike. Money markets still price in an over 50% chance of a quarter-end rate increase, particularly amid renewed US-Iran tensions. President Donald Trump announced plans to reinstate a blockade on Iranian vessels in the Strait of Hormuz and sought compensation from nations benefiting from US security efforts in the region.

### 2026-07-14 20:48:02  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #S&P500 #Impact bullish #DXY #Impact bearish #XAUUSD

【U.S. June CPI below expectations as energy prices retreat】U.S. consumer prices fell more than expected in June, recording the largest monthly drop since April 2020 as a sharp fall in energy eased recent inflationary pressure. Headline CPI declined 0.4% m/m (seasonally adjusted), taking the y/y rate to 3.5%; economists had penciled in -0.2% m/m and 3.8% y/y. Core CPI (ex food and energy) was unchanged m/m, with the 12-month rate at 2.6% versus market expectations of +0.2% m/m and 2.9% y/y. The energy index plunged 5.7% m/m (still +15.7% y/y); gasoline and fuel oil each fell more than 9%. Services inflation excluding energy eased noticeably—a key Fed-watch category—with housing up just 0.1% and transportation services down 0.3%. Food rose 0.2% m/m; new vehicle prices were flat; used cars and trucks fell 0.2%; apparel declined 0.6%.

US Futures Rebound on Soft CPI — Futures tracking US stock indices rebounded on Tuesday after softer inflation data erased the risk of a Federal Reserve rate hike this month. Contracts for the S&P 500 swung to 0.3% higher and those for the Nasdaq 100 gained nearly 1%, while the Dow erased a 500 point deficit to set up a flat open. The US CPI fell more than expected and the core gauge surprisingly refrained from increasing, easing concerns that higher underlying price growth would force the Fed to start hiking rates by their next meeting. This was magnified by another rally in energy prices after the US and Iran maintained strikes against each other. Chip producers rebounded from yesterday's selloff with Micron and Sandisk adding 5% each premarket. On the other hand, the country's largest banks were lower. JPMorgan fell 2.5% despite posting its strongest profit level ever, and Bank of America dropped 0.8% despite beating earnings. Likewise, Wells Fargo sank 2% even though its earnings report was also seen as a beat.

### 2026-07-14 20:42:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Inflation Rate MoM #Importance 1

US Consumer Prices Drop For First Time Since 2020 — US consumer prices fell 0.4% month-over-month in June 2026, following a 0.5% rise in May and exceeding market expectations of a 0.1% drop. This marked the first monthly decline since May 2020 and the largest since April 2020, driven primarily by a 5.7% drop in energy prices after increases of 3.9% in May, 3.8% in April, and 10.9% in March. Additional downward pressure came from motor vehicle insurance, communication, apparel, medical care, and used cars and trucks. Meanwhile, food prices rose 0.2%, with both food at home and food away from home increasing by the same margin. Other rising indexes included recreation, household furnishings and operations, and personal care. The core CPI, which excludes food and energy, remained unchanged, below forecasts of a 0.2% rise.

【我國將重構土地儲備的底層邏輯】隨著城鎮化和城市發展進入存量時代，實施25年的土地儲備制度將不斷創新改革，實現生產、生活、生態兼顧的底層邏輯重構。自然資源部副部長張文彤指出，土地儲備曾被稱為“經營城市”，關注點集中在土地本身、單一地塊以及土地自身的經濟價值特別是即期價值迅速實現。隨著城鎮化和城市發展由增量時代進入存量時代，城市內涵式發展要求土地儲備制度創新改革，從過去注重土地資源儲備，向土地、林礦等多種類轉變，既推動經濟發展，也推動生態文明建設；從單宗地塊向片區式各類資源有效組合轉變，在一定區域範圍內系統提高人居環境水平。土地儲備工作將從相對追求經濟價值，向追求經濟價值、社會價值、生態價值系統性轉變；不僅關注“淨地”自身產生的作用和價值，更關注透過土地儲備進一步提高基礎設施水平、完善城市功能，達到生產、生活、生態的高度統一，提供更多就業崗位、良好人居環境。（新華社）

### 2026-07-14 20:40:08  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #Treasury Yields #S&P500 #Impact bullish #WTI #DXY #Impact bearish

【U.S. CPI posts first monthly decline since 2020; core flat】U.S. consumer prices fell 0.4% MoM in June — the first monthly drop since 2020 — and rose 3.5% YoY, the Bureau of Labor Statistics said Tuesday. Core CPI (ex food and energy) was unchanged MoM and rose 2.6% YoY. The report said a retreat from the peak energy-price shock linked to the Iran war drove gasoline lower, while declines in apparel and used-car prices and a large drop in motor-vehicle insurance restrained core inflation. The print eases some near-term pressure on Fed tightening ahead of the Fed’s month-end meeting; investors pared bets on a July rate hike, U.S. equity futures rose and Treasury yields fell. Renewed U.S.-Iran hostilities lifting oil prices could, however, prolong conflict-driven inflationary effects.

### 2026-07-14 20:37:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 0

DXY Falls after Inflation Report — The dollar index fell more than 0.5% to around 100.7 on Tuesday after softer-than-expected US inflation reduced expectations of Federal Reserve rate hikes. Annual consumer inflation slowed to 3.5% in June from 4.2% in May, below forecasts of 3.8%, as lower energy prices helped ease overall price pressures. Core inflation also moderated to 2.6%, while monthly consumer prices fell 0.4%, marking the first monthly decline since 2020. The data offset recent hawkish remarks from Federal Reserve Chair Kevin Warsh, who reiterated the central bank's commitment to restoring price stability and stressed that policymakers have no tolerance for persistently elevated inflation. Meanwhile, renewed geopolitical tensions limited the dollar's decline after the interim US-Iran peace agreement unraveled. The US resumed strikes on Iran and reinstated a naval blockade, while Tehran launched fresh attacks on shipping in the Strait of Hormuz, reviving concerns over global energy supplies.

### 2026-07-14 20:19:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Poland's Trade Deficit Narrows in May — Poland's trade deficit narrowed to €4.5 billion in May 2026 from €5.0 billion in the same month a year earlier, as export growth outpaced imports. Exports rose 5.2% year-on-year, driven by higher shipments of raw silver, refined copper, and computer equipment, while exports of petroleum products also increased significantly. However, weaker sales from the automotive sector continued to weigh on overall export growth. Imports increased at a slower pace of 3.4%, despite a nearly 70% surge in oil import prices. Higher purchases of computers parts and components supported import growth, although lower imports of passenger cars helped limit the overall increase.

### 2026-07-14 20:18:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Current Account #Importance 1

Poland Current Account Gap Smaller than Expected — Poland posted a current account gap of €1,071 million in May 2026, narrowing from €1,168 million a year earlier and coming in below market expectations of a €1,674 million deficit. The goods shortfall shrank to €1,182 million from €1,637 million in May 2025, as the rate of increase in exports (+5.5%) outpaced that of imports (+3.7%). At the same time, the services surplus rose slightly to €3,379 million from €3,361 million, mainly due to positive balances in other services and transport services. On the other hand, the primary income deficit widened to €3,160 million from €2,843 million; and the secondary income gap went up to €108 million from €49 million.

### 2026-07-14 20:11:09  #公司 #市場 #觀點 #國際

【高盛股票交易業務收入再創華爾街紀錄 投行、資管等領域均表現亮眼】在股指持續上漲、人工智慧和中東戰爭相關市場波動持續之際，高盛集團股票交易業務收入重新整理其自身創出的華爾街紀錄，達到74.2億美元。高盛第二季度業績顯示，其股票業務連續第三個季度創下歷史最高紀錄。僅過去三個月的收入，就超過該業務2019年全年四個季度的總和。高盛週二在宣告中表示，股票業務收入同比增長72%，融資以及對安排押注獲利共同推動了這一增長。利率交易部門在第一季度表現令人失望後也超過預期；投資銀行部門在併購諮詢和承銷業務推動下，錄得2021年以來最高費用收入。高盛公佈的利率交易收入為45.9億美元。投行業務費用收入總計34億美元，超過彭博彙總的分析師共識預估。高盛股票交易收入再創新高，證實了彭博新聞社6月的報道。這一紀錄意味著高盛當季表現極為強勁，不過摩根大通的股票交易員錄得更大增幅。摩根大通週二早些時候公佈，其股票交易收入增長86%，至60.3億美元。高盛執行長蘇德巍6月表示，市場中“貪婪多於恐懼”。高盛投行團隊在第二季度牽頭了SpaceX創紀錄的首次公開募股以及Alphabet Inc.的股票融資，在排行榜上大幅領先同行。該行股票承銷業務收入較上年同期躍升130%。資料顯示，高盛在併購市場的份額超過三分之一，今年已為1萬億美元交易提供諮詢，達到任何銀行有記錄以來最快速度。高盛資產管理部門也在快速增長。該部門第二季度所管理資產規模為40.4億美元，較上年同期增加逾7億美元。業績還顯示，高盛淨營收達到創紀錄的203億美元。支出也有所上升，但員工薪酬增速低於收入增速。收入同比增長39%，薪酬增長30%。

### 2026-07-14 20:03:24  #焦點 #國際

【特朗普重置對伊60天軍事行動時限 停火協議不足一月即廢止】據報道，特朗普已於上週五致函國會，通報美軍重啟針對伊朗的軍事打擊。依據相關法案，自該通報之日起，五角大樓可在無需國會另行審批的前提下，在中東開展軍事行動60天，這相當於重新計算美方軍事行動的授權時限。

據報道援引這封總統信函，特朗普在信中寫明：本次打擊行動不會投入地面作戰部隊，軍事打擊具備有限性、針對性，方案經過周密規劃與執行，將最大限度避免平民傷亡。

在美國中央司令部官宣新一輪打擊前不久，特朗普做客節目時表態更為強硬。他在週一直言：“今晚我們會對伊朗發動猛烈打擊，明日同樣重拳出擊，伊朗對此完全無力反制。”美軍中央司令部宣佈，本次海上封鎖將於美國東部時間當日16時正式生效。

此番舉措徹底廢止了不到一個月前特朗普與伊朗簽署諒解備忘錄所達成的停火協議，該備忘錄約定設立60天休戰視窗期，用於雙方推進和平談判。

當被主持人問及這份停火協議從一開始就註定破裂時，特朗普坦言，他自始至終只將這份諒解備忘錄視作一次試探。“這本來就是一場試探，我們原本也無法預判結果。說白了，和不守規矩的對手打交道，諒解備忘錄本就沒有多少約束力；即便是和守信用的一方簽約，它本質也只是一份意向備忘，效力十分有限。”

特朗普補充道：“伊朗沒有遵守這份試探性協議。雙方此前曾多次敲定協議條款，我當時就篤定伊朗一定會違約，事實也的確如此，他們從頭到尾沒有履約。”

【中達安：終止向特定物件發行股票事項】中達安公告，公司於2026年7月14日召開第五屆董事會第十六次會議，審議透過了《關於終止向特定物件發行股票事項並撤回申請檔案的議案》，同意公司終止本次向特定物件發行股票事項並撤回申請檔案。公司於2025年6月25日召開第五屆董事會第六次會議、第五屆監事會第四次會議，並於2025年8月6日召開2025年第三次臨時股東大會，審議透過了關於公司2025年度向特定物件發行A股股票等相關議案。終止本次向特定物件發行股票事項是綜合考量再融資市場環境發生較大變化等客觀情況，經與相關各方充分溝通、審慎分析後作出的決策，不會對公司的正常生產經營活動造成重大不利影響。

US Futures Extend Pullback Ahead of CPI — Futures tracking US stock indices fell for a second session on Tuesday as concerns of higher interest rates lifted yields ahead of fresh inflation data. Contracts tracking the S&P 500 fell around 0.3% and those for the Dow slipped 500 points. Meanwhile, chip producers halted their selloff to stabilize the Nasdaq 100. Energy prices climbed further as the Iran struck tankers crossing the Strait of Hormuz and the US signaled it will tighten its blockade. The developments followed hawkish statements from FOMC Governor Waller, warning that higher rates may be needed to tame higher core inflation. AI hyperscalers were sharply lower premarket, with Microsoft and Oracle dropping around 3% each, while SpaceX fell 1% to erase nearly all its post-IPO gains. In turn JPMorgan fell 2.5% despite posting its strongest profit level ever, and Bank of America dropped 0.8% despite beating earnings. Likewise, Wells Fargo sank 2% even though its earnings report was also seen as a beat.

### 2026-07-14 19:24:43  #公司 #市場 #觀點 #國際

【摩根大通季度利潤創紀錄新高 股票交易收入達到60億美元】摩根大通公佈有史以來最高季度利潤，股票交易業務收入遠超分析師預期，加上長期持有的Visa Inc.股份帶來46億美元收益，共同推動業績創下新高。根據摩根大通週二釋出的宣告，第二財季股票交易收入同比增長86%，達到60.3億美元。這不僅超過彭博調查分析師中的最高預期，也推動總交易收入增至121億美元，超過今年第一季度創下的歷史最高紀錄。作為美國最大銀行，摩根大通與多數主要競爭對手一同公佈財報。分析師預計，在市場波動加劇的帶動下，自唐納德·特朗普贏得2024年總統大選以來持續火熱的股票交易業務，有望再度迎來一個豐收季度。儘管幾乎所有業務表現均好於預期，執行長傑米·戴蒙仍對未來前景保持謹慎。戴蒙在宣告中表示，“包括地緣政治緊張局勢和戰爭、頑固通脹、全球財政赤字高企以及資產價格處於高位在內的多重風險，正如同板塊運動般在表面之下不斷積聚。我們無法預測這些力量最終將如何演變。它們或許仍可控，但一旦發生變化或相互碰撞，也可能引發重大沖擊。”摩根大通股價今年截至週一累計上漲3.8%，但在紐約盤前交易中下跌2.6%。

【截至2026年6月底全國機動車保有量4.76億輛】記者7月14日從公安部交通管理局獲悉，據統計，截至2026年6月底，全國機動車保有量達4.76億輛，其中汽車3.71億輛；機動車駕駛人5.67億人，其中汽車駕駛人5.33億人。2026年上半年，全國新註冊登記機動車1581萬輛。其中，汽車新註冊登記1051萬輛，摩托車新註冊登記511萬輛。統計資料顯示，新能源汽車保有量佔比攀升，新註冊登記新能源汽車佔比近半。截至6月底，全國新能源汽車保有量達4897萬輛，佔汽車總量的13.19%，較去年同期提高2.92個百分點。其中，純電動汽車保有量3367.5萬輛，佔新能源汽車總量的68.77%。上半年新註冊登記新能源汽車519.5萬輛，佔汽車新註冊登記量的49.42%，較去年同期提高4.45個百分點。上半年全國共辦理機動車轉讓登記業務1852萬筆。其中，辦理汽車轉讓登記業務1705萬筆。全國異地辦理交易登記二手小客車業務345萬筆，同比增長11.98%。二手車異地交易登記改革措施在便利群眾企業辦事、促進二手車流通方面效果明顯。截至6月底，全國機動車駕駛人數量達5.67億人，其中汽車駕駛人數量為5.33億人，佔駕駛人總數的94.04%。上半年全國新領證駕駛人數量1304萬人，同比增長3.66%。面對網辦業務需求持續上漲，各地公安交管部門積極推行補換領牌證等交管業務“足不出戶”網上辦。全國網上辦理補換領駕駛證和行駛證、發放臨時號牌等業務5566萬筆，同比增長3.17%。（新華社）

【王滬寧會見越南共產黨代表團】中共中央政治局常委、全國政協主席王滬寧14日在京會見由越共中央政治局委員、中央內政部部長黎明智率領的越南共產黨代表團。王滬寧表示，習近平總書記同蘇林總書記保持密切戰略溝通。今年4月蘇林總書記成功訪華，同習近平總書記達成新的重要共識，為兩黨兩國關係和世界社會主義事業發展提供戰略指引。中方願同越方一道，落實好兩黨兩國最高領導人重要共識，推動中越命運共同體建設持續走深走實。中國全國政協願為此作出積極貢獻。黎明智祝賀中國共產黨成立105週年，表示越方欽佩習近平總書記提出系列全球倡議和帶領中國不斷前進的領導力，願共同落實好兩黨兩國最高領導人共識，借鑑中國特色社會主義理論，構建越中命運共同體。王東峰等參加會見。（新華社）

### 2026-07-14 19:07:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Rises Past $87 — Brent crude oil climbed more than 4% to around $87 per barrel on Tuesday, reaching its highest level in almost a month after the collapse of the US-Iran truce reignited concerns over supply disruptions. The rally followed a 9.6% surge in the previous session as fresh attacks targeted shipping in the Strait of Hormuz. The United Arab Emirates said two of its tankers were struck while transiting the waterway, highlighting growing risks to one of the world's busiest energy routes. The attacks came after the US resumed its blockade on Iranian oil exports, launched new strikes against Iran, and proposed charging vessels protected by the US Navy. Supply concerns also intensified as Saudi Arabia intercepted missiles launched by Yemen's Houthis, while Washington backed tougher sanctions on Russian energy exports. The renewed escalation has raised fears of prolonged disruptions to global oil supplies and higher geopolitical risk premiums.

### 2026-07-14 18:58:26  #公司 #國際

【阿聯酋石油公司兩艘油輪遭襲致1死8傷】阿聯酋阿布扎比國家石油公司14日確認，其旗下的兩艘超大型原油運輸船“蒙巴薩”號和“巴希婭”號當天凌晨在透過霍爾木茲海峽時遭飛行物襲擊。阿聯酋通訊社報道稱，該事件已造成1人死亡、8人受傷。阿布扎比國家石油公司當天發表宣告說，兩艘油輪均在襲擊中遭到嚴重損壞，宣告強烈譴責針對民用船舶及其海員的襲擊。阿聯酋國防部當天發表宣告譴責此次襲擊，稱其嚴重違反國際法，並表示阿聯酋軍方已作好充分準備應對任何威脅。阿聯酋外交部當天也發表宣告說，以商業航運為目標、將霍爾木茲海峽作為經濟脅迫或訛詐工具的做法不可接受，並稱其直接威脅地區穩定及全球能源和貿易安全。另據英國海上貿易行動辦公室14日釋出的兩份通報，有兩艘油輪在沿霍爾木茲海峽南部航道向外航行時被導彈擊中，事發地位於阿曼利邁東南約13海里處。知情人士透露，這兩艘遭襲油輪應為上述阿聯酋方面確認的那兩艘。美伊諒解備忘錄達成後，海峽形成兩條通行航道，分別為伊朗管控的北部航道和靠近阿曼一側的南部航道，美軍在南部航道提供通航協助。（新華社）

Most sectors saw increased selling pressure. HCL Tech (-4.4%) was the top laggard after its unchanged FY27 guidance overshadowed stronger-than-expected quarterly results. 【歐洲天然氣升至3月以來最高 美國擬對霍爾木茲海峽通行收費】由於美國總統唐納德·特朗普要求經霍爾木茲海峽通行的船舶支付費用，引發市場對中東能源運輸恢復前景的新一輪擔憂，歐洲天然氣價格升至3月以來最高水平。歐洲天然氣基準期貨連續第二個交易日上漲，盤中一度漲4.8%。特朗普表示，美國將成為霍爾木茲海峽的“守護者”，恢復對伊朗船隻的封鎖，並對其他所有貨物收取20%的費用，以換取其安全通行保障。分析師稱，以一船標準液化天然氣貨物計算，這意味著每次通行需支付約1200萬至1500萬美元。中東戰爭已導致相當一部分能源運輸中斷，其中包括約佔全球五分之一的液化天然氣供應。歐洲正面臨在下一個冬季到來前補充天然氣庫存的艱鉅任務，而近期衝突升級又進一步推高了本已有限的全球天然氣供應成本。與此同時，歐洲還需與亞洲等地區爭奪LNG貨源，並面臨高溫天氣推升能源需求帶來的額外壓力。截至阿姆斯特丹時間中午12點23分，歐洲天然氣基準荷蘭近月期貨價格上漲4.7%，報每兆瓦時53.69歐元。

### 2026-07-14 18:45:50  #宏觀

【財政部、應急管理部緊急預撥4.3億元中央自然災害救災資金支援廣西、浙江、河北、遼寧等10省（區、市）做好防汛防颱風應急搶險救災工作】近期，受颱風影響，我國華東、華南、華北、東北等多區域發生強降雨天氣，引發嚴重險情災情。為深入貫徹落實習近平總書記關於防汛救災工作的重要指示精神，7月14日，財政部、應急管理部緊急預撥4.3億元中央自然災害救災資金，支援廣西、浙江、河北、遼寧、天津、黑龍江、江蘇、安徽、江西、四川等10省（區、市）開展防汛防颱風應急搶險救災工作。其中，一是在前期預撥1.9億元基礎上，追加預撥1.5億元，支援廣西繼續重點做好受災群眾安置、過渡期生活救助以及倒損民房修復等工作，推動災區儘快恢復正常生產生活秩序；二是預撥2.8億元，支援其餘9省（市）統籌做好搜救轉移安置受災人員、排危除險等應急處置、開展次生災害隱患排查和應急整治等，最大限度減少災害損失。 財政部要求上述10省（區、市）財政廳（局）抓緊將資金撥付災區，加強救災資金監管，確保救災資金全部用於災區和受災群眾，切實保障人民群眾生命財產安全。 （財政部）

### 2026-07-14 18:42:29  #宏觀

【六部門釋出《固體廢物汙染防治“十五五”規劃》】生態環境部、國家發展改革委等六部門今天聯合印發《固體廢物汙染防治“十五五”規劃》。這是我國首部固體廢物汙染防治五年專項規劃，為全面推進美麗中國建設劃定了“十五五”時期的治汙路線圖。《規劃》提出了未來五年的總體目標：到2030年，重點領域固體廢物專項整治取得明顯成效，固體廢物歷史堆存量得到有效管控，非法傾倒處置高發態勢得到遏制，固體廢物綜合治理能力和水平顯著提升，固體廢物資訊化監管“一張網”實現重點領域全覆蓋，建成一批“無廢城市”。其中危險廢物填埋處置量佔比由2025年約13%降至10%以內，重點一般工業固體廢物貯存填埋量佔比由約56%降至30%以內，危險廢物相關單位全過程資訊化監管覆蓋率要達到100%。非法傾倒和非法處置固體廢物事件發生率指標，到2030年要大幅降低。《規劃》緊盯痛點攻堅，全鏈條智慧監管成為亮點，在開展非法傾倒處置、溶洞垃圾、磷石膏庫等重點領域專項整治中，運用衛星遙感、人工智慧建立智慧監測網路。對危險廢物開展全過程環境管理能力建設，推進“五即”，即產生、即包裝、即稱重、即打碼、即入庫規範化建設，強化危險廢物“一碼貫通”實時可追溯資訊化監管，全面實現危險廢物運輸車輛軌跡實時追蹤。針對新能源退役裝置、廢棄電器電子產品等新興領域，將加快完善回收處理體系，並支援200個左右城市開展“無廢城市”建設。專家介紹，我國每年產生固體廢物超過110億噸、歷年堆存工業固體廢物約300億噸，固體廢物汙染點多、面廣，《固體廢物汙染防治“十五五”規劃》的出臺，必將推動固體廢物汙染防治工作治難點、補短板、強弱項（央視新聞）

【Six ministries' solid-waste plan targets hazardous-waste landfill share below 10% by 2030】Six ministries issued China’s Solid Waste Pollution Prevention “15th Five-Year” Plan, setting 2030 targets: reduce hazardous-waste landfill disposal share from about 13% in 2025 to under 10%; cut storage-and-landfill share of priority general industrial solid waste from about 56% to below 30%; achieve 100% full-process digital supervision coverage for hazardous-waste units; and sharply lower incidence of illegal dumping and illegal disposal. The plan also calls for containment of historical stockpiles, materially upgraded overall solid-waste management capacity, integrated digital regulatory coverage across key areas, and construction of a number of zero-waste cities.

### 2026-07-14 18:18:18  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #US Economy #DXY #Impact mixed #US10Y #WTI

【US small-business confidence up in June; inflation remains top concern】US small-business optimism rose 2.1 points to 97.4 in June, near the 52-year average of 98.0, the NFIB said. The share of owners naming inflation as the single most important problem climbed to its highest level in nearly two years, and a larger number of firms reported raising average selling prices. The NFIB uncertainty index eased 2 points to 89 but remains well above its long-run average of 68. The NFIB said the improvement and lower uncertainty likely reflected a temporary pullback in gasoline after a fragile US–Iran ceasefire; the truce later collapsed following tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz and reciprocal military strikes, keeping fuel-price and geopolitical risk elevated. NFIB chief economist Bill Dunkelberg said lower fuel costs have provided relief for businesses and consumers and firms expect operating conditions to improve over the next six months, though challenges persist.

### 2026-07-14 18:10:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #NFIB Business Optimism Index #Importance 2

US Small Business Optimism Rebounds — The US NFIB Small Business Optimism Index increased to 97.4 in June 2026, the highest in four months, compared to 95.3 in May and forecasts of 95.8. Expectations for better business conditions and real sales improved substantially. 21% of business owners cited inflation as their single most important business problem, up 3 points from May and marking the highest reading since October 2024. Inflation ranks as the top problem. Still, the share of owners planning to raise prices in the near future declined. Also, the net percent of owners expecting better business conditions in the next six months rose 10 points to a net 13%. “Lower fuel costs provide welcome relief for businesses as well as consumers, with firms anticipating improved operating conditions over the next six months. While there have been improvements in the overall environment, high interest rates and modest economic growth are causing owners to approach hiring and capital spending with caution”, said NFIB Chief Economist.

【美銀調查：全球股票多頭應考慮適當減倉 做多半導體股成最擁擠交易】美國銀行最新基金經理調查顯示，近期大舉買入股票的全球投資者應考慮適當降低持倉。美國銀行策略師表示，資產配置者的樂觀情緒已升至極高水平，這通常被視為市場的一個警示訊號。調查顯示，現金持有比例已從上月佔資產的4.1%降至3.6%的"極低水平"，而美股配置比例則升至2024年12月以來最高，淨超配24%。由邁克爾·哈奈特領銜的團隊在報告中寫道，“美國銀行牛熊指標升至9.4的極度看漲水平，意味著投資者應降低股票及高貝塔資產的配置。”該指標的取值範圍為1至10。報告稱，"由於市場倉位已十分樂觀，風險資產今夏進一步上漲的空間可能受到限制。"

### 2026-07-14 17:48:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Mining Production #Importance 1

South Africa Mining Activity Suffers Surprise Setback — Mining production in South Africa slipped by 5.4% year-on-year in May 2026, following a downwardly revised 8% jump in April and worse than the expected 1.5% rise. The decline marked the first contraction in mining activity in six months and the steepest since February 2025. Iron ore was the largest negative contributor, declining 12.7% and subtracting 2.1 percentage points, followed by coal (-6.1%; -1.5 percentage points) and platinum group metals (PGMs), which fell 4.4%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, mining output dropped by 5.2% in May, after a downwardly revised 3.1% advance in the month before. Mining production shrank by 1.7% in the three months ended May 2026 compared with the previous three months.

### 2026-07-14 17:40:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Lumber Slips From Two-Week High — Lumber prices slipped below $620 per thousand board feet after reaching a two-week high of $627 on July 8th, as sawmill capacity remained subdued amid changing trade flows. US sawmill output declined for a second consecutive quarter, while production capacity fell 6% from a year earlier. American logs are pouring into China after lifting its pest-control ban, while US lawmakers push for policies favoring hardwood lumber exports over raw logs, arguing that raw material shipments reduce domestic feedstock and displace workers, which contributed to a 48.3% drop in lumber output since 2018. High borrowing and labor costs are also weighing on the housing market, dampening construction lumber demand. Canada is simultaneously urging the US to lift softwood lumber tariffs, with Premier David Eby calling on Ottawa to treat the dispute as a national priority, arguing that cumulative duties have forced mills, particularly in British Columbia, to scale back or shut down.

【National flood control headquarters elevates Jilin emergency response from Level IV to Level III】Typhoon Bavi has produced sustained heavy rainfall in Jilin province. The Songhua River's Jilin section generated the first flood event of 2026, and the Meihe, a Huifa River tributary, recorded its largest flood since monitoring began. Forecasts for July 14–15 call for continued heavy to torrential rain in central-eastern and eastern Jilin; the Huifa River is expected to exceed warning levels along its length. Authorities warn of high risk for river floods, flash floods, geological hazards, small- and medium-reservoir failures and urban inundation. The national flood control headquarters upgraded Jilin's flood emergency response to Level III at 16:00 on July 14; previously dispatched teams are on-site providing guidance.

【關志鷗在武漢調研時強調 堅持以科技創新為引領 加快推動醫工交叉融合和生物醫藥產業高質量發展】7月14日，湖北省委書記關志鷗在武漢市調研醫工交叉融合和生物醫藥產業發展時強調，要深入學習貫徹習近平總書記關於發展新質生產力的重要論述和考察湖北重要講話精神，充分發揮我省科教、產業、人才、醫療等優勢，推動醫工交叉融合和生物醫藥產業高質量發展，為加快建成中部地區崛起重要戰略支點提供強勁動能。光谷國際腦機介面創新中心是武漢東湖新技術開發區與華中科技大學同濟醫學院附屬同濟醫院、腦機介面研究院共同建設的腦機介面產業創新驅動平臺。關志鷗來到創新中心，與入駐企業依瑞德、格林泰克、凝動醫療等負責人深入交流，詳細瞭解企業技術研發、成果轉化情況，察看產品應用展示。他指出，腦機介面是未來產業的重要方向，湖北在這個領域擁有良好的發展基礎。要進一步加強創新中心建設，更大力度培育和招引龍頭企業，孵化培育初創企業，強化全方位、全鏈條、全週期服務，促進更多創新成果高效轉化應用。省市區要搶抓機遇，圍繞強鏈補鏈延鏈，整合資源力量，凝聚創新合力，以“光谷速度”推動腦機介面產業加快發展。（湖北釋出）

【White House official: Trump 'very serious' about 20% fee on Strait of Hormuz transits】A White House official told Semafor that Trump is "very serious" about imposing a 20% fee on goods transiting the Strait of Hormuz, a plan he has pushed for months. He has long sought such a levy, queried in April why his administration "wouldn't" do it, and threatened to start charging in June if an agreement with Iran was not reached within 60 days. The official said Trump has not always acted on such ideas and until this week had resisted formally announcing the plan, partly due to opposition within his inner circle. 【險資加倉遇上券商力挺 紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）近5年回報超52%】7月14日，市場探底回升，三大股指集體收紅，滬指漲1.36%，創業板指漲3.43%。受行情提振，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）上漲1.15%，報1.139元，換手率2.7%，成交額9.21億元，居同類標的ETF首位。資金流向上，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）長期受資金青睞。近5個交易日資金淨流入6.2億，近20個交易日資金淨流入18.5億元，近60個交易日資金淨流入47.5億元。截至2026年7月13日，該ETF流通規模340.12億元。統計顯示，截至2026年一季度末，保險資金合計重倉持有A股641只，較2025年底淨增24只，持股數量淨增約42.4億股。銀行股為增持重點方向。多家頭部險企表示，將持續配置低估值、高股息、長期盈利前景良好的權益類品種，積極響應中長期資金入市要求，發揮保險資金“耐心資本”優勢，強化絕對收益導向，最佳化權益持倉結構，增強新會計準則下投資業績的長期穩定性。國信證券指出，總體來看，短期A股階段性再平衡，但出現風格逆轉的可能性不大。往後看，A股伺機向上趨勢仍不改，7月將是一個重要的觀察視窗。結構上，科技成長內部有望向低位擴散，關注醫藥、金融及資源品等。廣發證券認為，紅利屬於賠率安全墊較厚的資產。6月股息率升至約5.2-5.65%，擁擠度降至約-1.5~-1.1倍標準差，與科技資產的擁擠度極差達到極致。海外下半年流動性總體將會過峰趨緊，紅利作為短久期資產，對貼現率上升的敏感度仍較低。順價從矽基擴散至碳基，紅利權重行業（銀行、煤炭、電力）的盈利預期邊際改善，均處於“順價擴散”的受益路徑上。投資者可將紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）作為底倉配置，無股票賬戶的投資者也可透過其場外聯接基金進行配置（A類：007466；C類：007467；I類：022678；Y類：022951）。

【New York to temporarily bar construction of new large data centers】New York Governor Kathy Hochul’s office said she plans to sign an executive order on Tuesday to temporarily halt construction of large data centers for up to one year, effective immediately. The pause applies to facilities with electricity demand of 50 MW or more and is intended to buy time to draft rules that balance environmental objectives with grid reliability. The action makes New York the latest U.S. jurisdiction to curb AI-driven infrastructure growth; no other state has enacted a statewide pause, though dozens of U.S. counties have issued temporary bans and several states have proposed similar measures. Opponents cite risks to local power supply and rising residential utility costs, and some voters have sought to recall officials who support data center development.

【國內首個，京津冀聯合釋出小客車油改電碳普惠標準】近日，京津冀三地同步釋出《京津冀碳普惠專案減排量核算技術規範油改電小客車》區域協同地方標準（以下簡稱“標準”）。這是國內首個面向小客車油改電行為的跨區域碳普惠標準，統一了京津冀區域碳減排量核算規則，將綠色出行轉化為可核算、可交易、可激勵的碳資產，引導全民參與低碳轉型。 燃油小客車是交通大氣汙染物排放和碳排放重要來源之一，小客車也是三地跨區域通勤的重要交通工具。此次標準的釋出，統一了京津冀區域小客車油改電碳減排量核算規範，將“綠色行為”轉化為“綠色資產”，有助於推動形成綠色生產生活方式。(新京報)

【美軍稱將恢復對伊海上封鎖 伊朗打擊中東美軍基地】美軍13日宣佈，對伊朗發動“連續第三晚”的打擊行動，並將於美國東部時間14日恢復對伊朗的海上封鎖。伊朗方面則表示霍爾木茲海峽近期局勢升級的直接責任在美國，並對位於中東地區的美軍基地實施報復性打擊。

據美國方面13日訊息，美國總統特朗普已正式通知國會，伊朗戰事重新爆發。

美軍中央司令部13日說，美國東部時間當天16時45分，美軍開始對伊朗發動“連續第三晚”的打擊行動，在歷時5小時的行動中，美軍打擊了位於伊朗布什爾、恰巴哈爾港、阿巴斯港等地的多處軍事目標，美軍使用精確制導炸彈打擊了伊朗的海岸防禦系統、導彈和無人機基地以及海上軍事設施。

美軍中央司令部13日在社交媒體發文稱，根據特朗普的指示，美軍將於美國東部時間14日16時（北京時間15日4時）開始恢復對伊朗的海上封鎖行動。美國主導的聯合海上資訊中心13日也表示，美軍中央司令部將於上述時間開始執行針對所有伊朗港口和伊朗全部沿海區域的海上封鎖，封鎖措施不區分船舶懸掛的船旗。

特朗普13日在社交媒體上證實，美國將恢復對伊朗的海上封鎖，並將對所有經由霍爾木茲海峽運輸的貨物收取20%的費用。

針對特朗普上述說法，伊朗外交部長阿拉格齊14日在社交媒體發文說：“美國總統說得沒錯，誰保障霍爾木茲海峽的航運安全，誰就該收這筆服務費。伊朗一直是海峽的守護者，以後也是。不過20%確實太高了，我們會公道的。”

伊朗外交部發言人巴加埃13日表示，伊美諒解備忘錄有關霍爾木茲海峽的條款內容明確，不存在任意解釋空間，霍爾木茲海峽近期局勢升級的直接責任在美國。伊朗武裝部隊哈塔姆安比亞中央總部發言人13日則表示，絕不允許美國干涉霍爾木茲海峽的管理，伊朗武裝部隊將對美軍未經伊朗授權、擅自闖入指定航道、干擾商船和油輪通行的行為予以強力回擊。

伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊14日證實，兩艘巨型油輪被美國“惡意操縱”，關閉導航系統。它們無視霍爾木茲海峽航運安全控制中心的多次警告，危及該航道的航行安全並選擇穿越雷區，最終被伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊海軍擊中並失去動力。

與此同時，伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊還稱，其海軍和航空航天部隊用導彈和無人機攻擊了位於巴林的美國海軍第五艦隊，擊中該艦隊燃料庫，成功擊中並摧毀雷達系統以及“愛國者”防空系統，美國海軍第五艦隊的無人艇控制與監控中心也被徹底摧毀。（央視新聞）

【Solar supplied 25% of EU power in June, a record — Ember】Think tank Ember said solar accounted for 25% of EU power generation in June, a monthly record and the single largest generation source for the month, surpassing nuclear and natural gas. Ember said the share has risen from about 10% five years earlier as member states accelerated solar-panel installations. In Spain, a European leader in solar deployment, solar provided more than one-third of national electricity supply in June. Ember analyst Chris Rosslowe said solar has moved from a marginal role to an indispensable part of Europe’s power system as governments and consumers seek low-cost, easily deployed domestic generation.

### 2026-07-14 16:44:52  #MKT News

【SASAC convenes forum of central SOE scientists】On July 13 the State-owned Assets Supervision and Administration Commission (SASAC) held a forum with scientist representatives from central state-owned enterprises. The meeting said central SOEs are the backbone of China’s push to become a technologically strong nation and must shoulder the mission of achieving high‑level technological self‑reliance. It called for systematized R&D efforts anchored to major national strategies, for central SOEs to take on more national‑level major research projects and to deliver more indigenous, proprietary technologies. The meeting urged deeper integration of technological and industrial innovation, stronger collaborative innovation with universities, research institutes and upstream/downstream firms, and leadership in technology maturation, process optimization and product iteration. It called for stepped‑up talent development through more proactive, open and effective talent policies to cultivate outstanding young science and technology talent. The meeting stressed improving R&D input‑output efficiency, increasing the share of basic research in total R&D spending and aligning larger funding with efficiency gains; refining assessment and evaluation systems and deepening institutional reforms to prioritize innovation quality and effectiveness; and strengthening research ethics governance and technology security risk prevention to better balance development and security.

### 2026-07-14 16:43:36  #宏觀 #市場

【國務院國資委召開中央企業科技工作者代表座談會 深入學習貫徹習近平總書記重要講話精神 在推進高水平科技自立自強中衝鋒在前擔當主力】7月13日，國務院國資委召開中央企業科技工作者代表座談會，共同學習領會習近平總書記在國家科學技術獎勵大會、兩院院士大會、中國科協十一大上的重要講話精神，統一思想、凝聚共識、共謀發展。國務院國資委黨委書記、主任程福波代表國資委黨委向榮獲國家科學技術獎的獲獎者表示熱烈祝賀，向長期以來奮戰在國資央企科研一線的兩院院士和科技工作者表示誠摯慰問，強調要把學習貫徹習近平總書記重要講話精神，與貫徹落實習近平總書記關於國資央企科技創新工作的重要指示批示精神結合起來，牢記國資央企職責使命，增強敢為人先、攻堅克難動力，在推進高水平科技自立自強中衝鋒在前、擔當主力。國務院國資委黨委委員、副主任譚作鈞主持會議。國務院國資委黨委委員、副主任李鎮，秘書長出席會議。

會議認為，黨的十八大以來，以習近平同志為核心的黨中央高度重視科技創新工作。習近平總書記的重要講話，充分肯定了近年來我國科技事業取得的重大成就，深刻分析了科技發展面臨的新形勢，圍繞抓好黨中央關於“十五五”時期科技事業各項部署的落實提出明確要求，對兩院院士和廣大科技工作者提出殷切希望，為做好新時代科技工作指明瞭前進方向、提供了根本遵循。國資央企要牢記囑託、感恩奮進，深刻把握蘊含其中的戰略考量、思想內涵、目標要求和規律方法，增強科技報國的責任感使命感緊迫感，用實幹擔當不負黨中央的厚望重託。

### 2026-07-14 16:38:40  #其他

【教育部召開高校體育工作座談會】7月14日，教育部在浙江大學召開高校體育工作座談會，總結交流各地各高校深化高校體育改革、實施大學生體質提升專項行動實踐探索，推動高校體育高質量發展。教育部黨組成員、副部長、總督學王嘉毅出席會議並講話。

會議指出，近年來各地各高校大力踐行“健康第一”教育理念，全面深化高校體育改革，推動體育教學體系更加完善，治理格局不斷健全，條件保障更加堅實，校園賽事蓬勃開展，高校體育工作取得了新成效。

會議強調，要提高政治站位，深刻把握加強高校體育工作是踐行“健康第一”教育理念、促進五育並舉全面發展的應有之義，是全面深化教育改革、支撐教育高質量發展的必然要求，是推進強國建設、服務中國式現代化的迫切需要。要緊緊抓住大學生體質提升這一目標任務，堅持“一校一策”，層層壓實工作責任，持續最佳化體育課程體系，豐富有組織的課外體育活動供給，保障鍛鍊時長。要完善體質健康測試製度，構建過程性與結果性相結合的綜合評價體系，激發學生自覺鍛鍊的內生動力。要加強體育師資隊伍建設，持續改善體育場地設施條件，落實好經費投入保障，加強區域資源整合，推動高校體育工作提質增效。要強化體育文化浸潤，打造校園體育品牌，加強高校體育對外交流合作，守好學校體育安全底線。（教育部）

### 2026-07-14 16:32:48  #其他

【中汽協澄清：“新能源車平均車齡僅1.8年”是誤讀】7月14日，中國汽車工業協會後市場分會發布澄清宣告稱：近期，有媒體引用分會發布的《2025中國汽車後市場年度發展報告》中部分資料，釋出“新能源車平均車齡僅1.8年、消費者3-5年置換電車”等報道，相關內容引發市場誤讀。

中國汽車工業協會後市場分會就有關情況澄清說明稱：一、核心概念區分：“在用車平均車齡”≠“車輛置換週期”≠“車輛平均使用壽命”。報告中釋出的“在用車平均車齡”，具體指，截至2025年底，我國存量新能源車輛的平均新車售出時間，相當於人的平均年齡，而不是平均壽命。“車輛置換週期”是指車主從購車到主動換車的平均間隔時長，“車輛平均使用壽命”是新車售出後到報廢的平均使用年限，三者統計口徑、統計物件完全不同。網傳報道推導得出的“電車快速迭代更換”結論失真，屬於統計概念誤用。關於“車輛置換週期”“車輛平均壽命”的具體資料，請以官方統計口徑為準或參考行業其他權威報告調研結論。

二、資料客觀說明：報告顯示，傳統動力型別乘用車在用車平均車齡約8.2年，新能源乘用車普及時間短、近年銷量高速增長、新增上牌車輛佔比高，因此，整體存量新能源乘用車平均車齡約1.8年。該數值僅反映的是行業轉型發展現階段特徵，與當前市場情況一致，符合客觀事實和現實體感，不代表車主僅1.8年就更換車輛，也不代表車輛使用壽命為平均1.8年。

三、資料使用建議：汽車行業統計和調研資料具有特定的專業口徑和適用範圍，對於報告中資料標註解釋不夠到位所引發的誤讀，特此說明並深表歉意。請媒體和相關人士在引用和傳播過程中明確統計概念，客觀使用指標資料，以便公眾獲得準確、完整的資訊，避免誤讀誤解。（上證報）

【全球首顆大算力3D晶片釋出，今年已註冊4.5萬家晶片相關企業】據介面新聞報道，7月13日，東方算芯正式釋出全球首顆大算力3D晶片DF1000。這是全球首顆採用3D架構的大算力AI晶片，以全國產供應鏈為基礎，結合軟體定義近存計算與3D混合鍵合技術，在算力、訪存效率上實現關鍵突破，為國產AI晶片的發展提供了兼具創新性與可行性的全新路徑。企查查資料顯示，截至7月14日，國內現存晶片相關企業42.33萬家。近十年晶片相關企業註冊量呈整體增長態勢，2025年全年註冊量突破8萬家，同比增長19.91%至8.76萬家，創近十年註冊量新高。截至目前，今年已註冊4.5萬家。存量方面，從經營時間來看，成立1-3年的企業佔比最高，達29.63%。從地區分佈來看，晶片相關企業主要分佈在華南、華東兩地，分別佔比35.39%、33.26%。

### 2026-07-14 16:18:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Current Account #Importance 1

Czech Republic Current Account Deficit Widens in May — The Czech Republic’s current account deficit widened to CZK 38.3 billion in May 2026 from CZK 17.6 billion a year earlier, far exceeding market expectations of a CZK 25 billion deficit. This marked a return to deficit after five consecutive months of surpluses. The primary income account deficit widened to CZK 66.3 billion from CZK 49.8 billion in May 2025, while the secondary income account deficit also increased to CZK 7.9 billion from CZK 2.8 billion. At the same time, the goods account surplus narrowed to CZK 24.2 billion from CZK 26.5 billion. In contrast, the services account surplus expanded to CZK 11.7 billion from CZK 8.4 billion.

【阿聯酋兩艘油輪遇襲導致一名海員喪生 霍爾木茲海峽航運風險驟升】阿拉伯聯合大公國表示，兩艘油輪在穿越霍爾木茲海峽時遭到襲擊，一名海員喪生。這是自美國與伊朗達成的臨時和平協議於上週事實上接近破裂以來，該水道發生的最嚴重的商船遇襲事件。阿聯酋國防部表示，兩艘船隻遭到伊朗巡航導彈襲擊。阿布扎比國家石油公司子公司Adnoc L&S隨後發表宣告稱，油輪“Al Bahyah”號和“Mombasa B”號在霍爾木茲海峽航行期間遭到投射武器擊中，船體嚴重受損。宣告還表示，一名船員死亡，另有數名船員受傷。此次襲擊對石油市場影響重大，因為涉事方是自本輪衝突爆發以來保障非伊朗原油透過霍爾木茲海峽運輸的重要力量。其中一方是阿聯酋，該國上月原油產量和出口量大幅增長；另一方則是油輪船東Sinokor Group，該公司一直是阿聯酋原油出口的重要承運商。阿布扎比國家石油公司表示，遭襲的Sinokor油輪是透過定期租船協議承租使用。此次油輪遇襲以及海員死亡事件，將引發市場對於阿聯酋是否能夠繼續維持大規模原油運輸的擔憂。

### 2026-07-14 16:08:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Palestine #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Palestine CPI Plunges 40% in June — Consumer prices in Palestine plunged 40.04% year-on-year in June 2026, deepening from a 36.96% decline in May and marking the sharpest deflation since December last year. Across the territories, consumer prices fell 62.77% in the Gaza Strip, while inflation remained positive in the West Bank (1.31%) and Jerusalem (2.31%). The sharp decline continued to reflect the normalization of prices in Gaza following the exceptionally high levels recorded during the previous two years. On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 1.6% in June, following a 0.4% decline in May. This was driven by broad-based declines in gas (-67.31%), eggs (-25.04%), fresh vegetables (-14.75%), fresh chicken (-11.72%), potatoes and other tubers (-9.82%), dried vegetables (-9.58%), fresh fruit (-7.39%), diesel fuel (-7.19%), rice (-1.62%), and vegetable oils (-1.05%).

### 2026-07-14 16:03:58  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #US Economy #US10Y #Impact bullish #DXY #Impact bearish #WTI

【Analyst says US monthly CPI may turn negative, easing market fears of imminent Fed rate hike】Analyst Matthew Weller says whether inflation has peaked is the market’s key question ahead of June CPI. A partial reopening of the Strait of Hormuz in mid‑June — the first in three months — pushed energy prices sharply lower, and the lagged effects of Trump’s now‑expired "Liberation Day" tariffs appear largely priced in. Traders and economists now expect overall monthly CPI to post its first decline in over a year, which would temper fears of an imminent Fed hike; fed funds futures price a 35% chance of a rate increase at this month’s meeting. Last month’s meeting under new Fed chair Kevin Warsh took a relatively hawkish tone; while that hawkish view may be optimistic, an upside CPI surprise plus Warsh’s congressional appearances Tue‑Wed could still spark outsized market volatility.

【黑龍江省人大民族僑務外事委員會原主任委員蔡炳華被開除黨籍】中央紀委國家監委網站7月14日通報，據黑龍江省紀委監委訊息：日前，經黑龍江省委批准，黑龍江省紀委監委對黑龍江省人大民族僑務外事委員會原主任委員蔡炳華嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，蔡炳華喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠、不老實，對抗組織審查；無視中央八項規定精神，違規接受宴請，利用職權或職務上的影響為本人謀求特殊待遇；違背組織原則，不按規定報告個人有關事項，賣官鬻爵，任人唯親；廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮品、禮金；不正確履行職責，違規決策專案建設；將公權力異化為謀取私利的工具，大搞權錢交易，利用職務上的便利，在工作調轉、崗位調整、職務提拔、工程專案承攬等方面為他人謀取利益，並非法收受鉅額財物。蔡炳華嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經省紀委常委會會議研究並報省委批准，決定給予蔡炳華開除黨籍處分；按規定取消其享受的待遇；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

Softer market bets on ECB tightening have left scope for euro-area short-end rates to firm, underpinning the euro. However, a sustained rise in oil/energy prices could limit that upside. FTSE MIB Moves Lower — The FTSE MIB fell to around 52,300 on Tuesday, halting a three-day winning streak, as escalating tensions in the Middle East weighed on market sentiment. Crude prices extended their gains after renewed US-Iran strikes fueled concerns over global supply, while President Trump announced plans to levy transit fees on ships using the Strait of Hormuz. Losses were broad-based, led by financial stocks including Unicredit (-1.2%), Banco BPM (-1.6%), Mediobanca (-1.2%), Banca Mediolanum (-1.2%), BPER Banca (-1.7%), and Banca Monte dei Paschi di Siena (-1.0%). Meanwhile, Telecom Italia slipped 0.7%, despite an Italian court rejecting Inwit's urgent request to suspend the company's withdrawal from a tower lease agreement. Energy stocks continued to outperform, with Eni rising nearly 2% and Tenaris gaining about 1%, supported by higher oil prices.

### 2026-07-14 15:55:52  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

French Stocks Fall on Geopolitical Woes — The CAC 40 fell 0.8% to 8,295 on Tuesday, reversing the previous session’s gains as investors continued to weigh the impact of ongoing tensions in the Middle East. President Trump reimposed a US blockade on Iranian vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz and sought a 20% fee on all other cargo passing through the waterway. US forces are also expected to resume maritime patrols and restrictions around Iranian ports and coastal areas, a move that Iran views as a violation of the interim peace agreement and one that could prompt further attacks on commercial shipping. The developments drove oil prices sharply higher, stoking concerns over inflation and the prospect of tighter monetary policy. Meanwhile, attention was also focused on the start of the earnings season. Among decliners, luxury stocks led the losses, with LVMH (-2.1%), Hermès (-1.8%), and Kering (-2.4%) under pressure. Other notable laggards included Safran (-1.2%), EssilorLuxottica (-2.5%), and Société Générale (-1.4%).

【花旗：預計內銀次季收入增長維持韌性，看好大型銀行及區域性銀行】花旗發表報告，預計覆蓋的中國銀行2026年第二季收入增長維持韌性，將同比增長6.8%，較第一季7.4%有所放緩，主要受交易收益高基數影響，次季收入增長主要受以下因素支撐，包括企業貸款按年增長仍較為穩健，雖然較首季略有放緩；淨息差按季趨穩；手續費收入強勁增長。該行預計內銀資產質量將保持穩定，但將藉由強勁的收入增長多計提撥備以備不時之需，從而導致盈利預計按年增長2.9%，增長低於收入增長。該行預計大型銀行及區域性銀行表現將優於股份制銀行，主要得益於更好的企業貸款增長。四大內銀以及區域性銀行如南京銀行、寧波銀行的收入增長將更為強勁；寧波銀行、杭州銀行、常熟銀行的盈利增長將較快。在所涵蓋的銀行中，重農行、民生銀行次季業績有可能超出市場預期，而光大銀行則可能表現令人失望。

### 2026-07-14 15:39:13  #國際

【外交部：中方願同坦贊雙方共同推進好坦贊鐵路啟用和繁榮帶建設】7月14日，外交部發言人林劍主持例行記者會。 有記者提問，今天是坦贊鐵路全線正式通車50週年。請問中方如何評價坦贊鐵路的歷史意義，對坦贊鐵路未來有何設想? 林劍表示，坦贊鐵路是中非友誼的歷史豐碑。50多年前，中國在自身經濟十分困難的情況下毅然決定援建坦贊鐵路，有力支援地區國家民族解放和發展建設事業。中坦贊三國人民在建設坦贊鐵路過程中手挽手、肩並肩，共同鑄就了偉大的坦贊鐵路精神，為後人留下了寶貴的精神財富。 2024年9月，習近平主席同坦尚尼亞總統哈桑、尚比亞總統希奇萊馬共同見證簽署《坦贊鐵路啟用專案諒解備忘錄》，標誌著坦贊鐵路進入了新的歷史時刻。2025年11月，李強總理在訪問尚比亞期間，同尚比亞總統希奇萊馬、坦尚尼亞副總統恩欽比共同出席了坦贊鐵路啟用開工儀式，三國政府共同發表《關於攜手打造坦贊鐵路繁榮帶的聯合宣告》。 林劍表示，站在新的歷史起點上，中方願同坦贊雙方共同推進好坦贊鐵路啟用和繁榮帶建設，共同打造自由、發展、友誼、幸福、綠色、和諧之路，助力三國及地區國家共築現代化，為新時代全天候中非命運共同體建設注入新的不竭動力。（北京日報）

FTSE 100 Falls on Tuesday — The FTSE 100 edged lower on Tuesday as renewed US-Iran tensions pushed oil prices higher, with gains in energy and mining stocks outweighed by broader market weakness. President Donald Trump said the US would reinstate a blockade on Iranian vessels using the Strait of Hormuz and seek payments on all other cargo transiting the waterway. Banking stocks declined, with HSBC, Lloyds Banking and Barclays down around 0.6%, NatWest losing 0.9%, and Standard Chartered falling more than 1%. Defence names also weakened, with Rolls-Royce down 2% and BAE Systems slipping 0.6%, while Unilever, BAT, GSK and AstraZeneca also traded lower. AstraZeneca announced an exclusive global licensing agreement for its lung cancer treatment Zegfrovy in a deal worth up to $1.5 billion. UK Gilt Yields Surge on Rate Hike Bets — UK 10-year gilt yields climbed above 5%, reaching their highest level since May 21, as the Middle East conflict stoked fears that rising energy prices could drive inflation and interest rates higher. Two-year gilt yields also rose above 4.4%, their highest in nearly two months, after gaining nearly 13 basis points on Monday. The US military continued strikes against Iran after President Donald Trump reinstated a blockade on Iranian shipping and proposed a 20% fee to guard the Strait of Hormuz, heightening uncertainty over energy flows. Markets responded by pricing in further Bank of England rate hikes, nearly fully expecting two increases in 2026, with a September hike now fully priced in. In the US, Fed Governor Christopher Waller warned that the central bank may need to raise rates "in the near term" if inflation remains above the 2% target. Investors now await remarks from Federal Reserve Chair Kevin Warsh and US inflation data later today.

### 2026-07-14 15:34:52  #MKT News #Important #005930.KS

【Samsung Electronics exploring potential US ADR listing, sources say】Samsung Electronics is in preliminary talks to issue American Depositary Receipts (ADRs) in the US, sources say. The company has held early discussions with several banks but has made no decision and will closely monitor volatile memory-chip peers before proceeding. If pursued, structuring a US listing could be complicated by Samsung’s diversified business mix and ongoing labor disputes. Sources say Samsung has considered ADRs before but shelved the idea; SK Hynix’s recent successful US listing has renewed interest. Samsung shares are up about 120% year-to-date, pushing market value above $1 trillion; that rally already reflects elevated earnings growth expectations. Samsung’s preliminary results beat forecasts last week but the subsequent sharp share drop highlights the high bar for investor satisfaction. German Yields Surge on Rate Hike Expectations — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield rose above 3.1%, hitting its highest level since May 21, as the Middle East conflict raised concerns that higher energy prices could fuel inflation and interest rates. The two-year bond yield, more sensitive to policy rate expectations, also reached 2.8%, its highest since July 2024. The US military continued strikes against Iran after President Donald Trump reinstated a blockade on Iranian shipping and proposed a 20% fee to guard the Strait of Hormuz, increasing uncertainty over energy flows. Markets responded by betting on further European Central Bank rate hikes, with money markets pricing in a deposit rate of 2.70% by December, up from the current 2.25%, and fully expecting a September hike. In the US, Fed Governor Christopher Waller warned that the central bank may need to raise rates "in the near term" if inflation remains above the 2% target. Investors now await remarks from Federal Reserve Chair Kevin Warsh and US inflation data later today.

【外交部：“南海仲裁案”本質上是一出披著法律外衣的政治鬧劇】7月14日，外交部發言人林劍主持例行記者會。 有記者提問，據瞭解，南海安全圓桌對話7月13日在香港特區舉行，外交部與會代表再次駁斥有關“南海仲裁案”的謬論。發言人能否介紹有關的情況？ 林劍指出，正如外交部代表在南海安全圓桌對話會上所表示的，“南海仲裁案”本質上是一出披著法律外衣的政治鬧劇。10年前，所謂的“仲裁庭”越權管轄、枉法裁決，作出非法無效的裁決。10年來，越來越多的有識之士看清“南海仲裁案”的真實面目，眾多國家實踐和國際司法機構對有關案件的處理也不同於“裁決”。 林劍表示，中國不接受、不參與“仲裁”，不接受、不承認“裁決”，正是以實際行動維護國際海洋秩序。“南海仲裁案”一紙非法“裁決”，不會改變中國對南海諸島及其附近海域擁有主權權利和管轄權的歷史和事實，不會動搖中國維護主權和海洋權益的決心和意志，不會影響中國同直接當事國透過談判協商解決有關爭議的政策和立場。中國將繼續同地區國家一道，維護南海的和平與安全，促進地區的繁榮與發展。（北京日報）

Sentiment was also supported by optimism over Hong Kong's IPO pipeline, though gains remained capped by geopolitical tensions and elevated oil prices. Shein stayed in focus after Executive Chairman Donald Tang stepped down ahead of its planned Hong Kong IPO, with founder and CEO Sky Xu assuming the chairman role. While the change is not expected to derail the listing, investors are watching corporate governance developments closely. Attention now turns to China's second-quarter GDP and other key economic data due on Wednesday. 【摸清164種礦產、森林覆蓋率25.09%！我國自然資源“家底”公佈】國務院新聞辦公室14日舉行新聞釋出會，介紹完善自然資源資產管理制度體系有關情況，並答記者問。釋出會上，有關負責人介紹，近年來，自然資源部持續開展自然資源統一調查監測，統籌實施自然資源基礎調查及各類專項調查，覆蓋土地、礦產、森林、草原、溼地、水、海洋、荒漠等各類資源以及國家公園等國土空間。在浙江、重慶等地空間數字化協同治理試點的基礎上，融合基礎地理資訊、調查監測、產權、規劃等資料，構建自然資源管理和國土空間規劃“一張圖”，動態掌握各類資源的狀況，不斷提升精細化和智慧化管理水平。牢牢守住了耕地保護紅線，耕地數量、質量、生態“三位一體”保護格局逐步形成；全面摸清了有查明資源儲量的164種礦產資源的數量、分佈和開發利用情況，夯實能源資源安全根基；全國森林覆蓋率達25.09%，森林蓄積量達到209.88億立方米，成為全球增綠最多最快的國家；我國管轄海域約300萬平方公里，大陸自然岸線保有率保持在35%以上，自然資源家底更加厚實。（央視新聞）

### 2026-07-14 15:08:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Norway #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Norway Producer Inflation Slows in June — Producer prices in Norway rose 14.9% year-on-year in June 2026, slowing from a 24.0% surge in May, which marked the strongest growth since September 2022. It was the fourth consecutive month of double-digit producer inflation, though the weakest reading in the current run. The moderation was largely driven by slower price growth for the extraction of oil and natural gas (15.2% vs. 35.1% in May) and energy goods (23.9% vs. 41.2%), reflecting easing energy-related cost pressures. Manufacturing producer inflation also eased to 8.6% from 9.4%. In contrast, inflation for electricity, gas and steam accelerated to 68.8% from 58.5%, remaining the strongest contributor to overall producer price growth. Excluding energy goods, producer prices increased 5.3%, easing from 5.7% in May, suggesting underlying inflationary pressures continued to moderate. On a monthly basis, producer prices fell 7.1%, the biggest drop since March 2025, and following a 1.8% decline in May.

### 2026-07-14 15:07:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Inflation Rate #Importance 3

US Inflation Rate Seen Falling for 1st Time in 4 Months — The annual inflation rate in the US is expected to ease to 3.8% in June 2026 from 4.2% in May, marking its first slowdown in four months after reaching its highest level since April 2023. On a monthly basis, the CPI is forecast to edge down 0.1%, which would be the first decline since May 2020, largely reflecting lower energy prices. Gasoline prices are estimated to have fallen by approximately 9% following the ceasefire between the US and Iran, alleviating inflationary pressures from the energy component. Moderate increases are expected in airfares and rents, while prices for services, particularly hotel and motel accommodations, are likely to rise amid increased demand related to the FIFA World Cup. Motor vehicle insurance prices are also expected to rebound. Meanwhile, core consumer prices likely rose 0.2% for a second consecutive month, leaving the annual core inflation rate unchanged at 2.9%.

Domestic data showed Australia's consumer sentiment rebounded in July but remained among the weakest readings in the survey’s 50-year history, underscoring vulnerability to global shocks. Business confidence, however, climbed to a four-month high in June. In main trading partner China, record June trade figures set the stage for Q2 GDP due Wednesday. 【JP Morgan: Tencent remains China internet’s highest-quality compounding growth stock, reiterates Overweight】JP Morgan reiterates Overweight on Tencent Holdings (00700.HK) and keeps its HKD690 target. The bank says Tencent’s durable user engagement, improving game and advertising monetisation, strong margins and balance sheet, and AI monetisation optionality make it the highest-quality compounding growth play in China’s internet sector. JP Morgan expects AI-related depreciation to begin in H2, and has cut its FY26 Non-IFRS EPS forecast by 5% to 28.47 yuan while raising FY26 capex to 200 bln yuan; the firm stresses these changes reflect heavier AI investment and depreciation, not a deterioration in core operations. JP Morgan projects Q2 revenue up about 9% YoY, with value-added services +5–6%, games +9–10%, advertising +18–19% and fintech ~+5%, broadly in line with market expectations.

It said 1H grid-delivered generation fell 3.3% YoY to 109.6 million MWh and average unit utilization hours fell 6.1% YoY to 3,554 hours, while market-priced sales share rose 9.8pp to 65.9%. Citigroup forecasts Q2 net profit down 7.8% YoY to RMB2.7bn, chiefly due to the higher share of market-priced sales, and said Guangdong’s Q2 spot-price rise should have limited impact because over 95% of market-priced volume is sold under long-term contracts. Hungary Construction Output at 4-Month Low — Hungary’s construction output fell by 10.7% year-on-year in May 2026, following a 2.6% rise in the previous month. This marked the weakest reading since January, as production in civil engineering works plunged 21.9% from a 13.9% increase, driven mainly by a 46.2% contraction in road and railway construction, which reduced the overall performance of the civil engineering segment by 38 percentage points. Meanwhile, output in building construction contracted at a slower pace at 4.2%, easing from a 6.2% decline in April, as a 12.4% increase in specialised construction activities, the industry's largest segment, partially offset a 19.1% drop in building construction and a 34.9% slump in civil engineering output. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, construction activity dropped by 6.5% in May, following a 6.9% increase in the preceding period. For the Janury–May period, the total volume of construction fell by 4.3% compared to the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-07-14 14:51:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Hungary Industrial Output Fall Confirmed at 0.4% — Industrial production in Hungary fell 0.4% year-on-year in May 2026, confirming preliminary estimates and reversing a 0.9% increase in the previous month. Manufacturing output, which accounts for 96% of total industrial production, was flat after rising 0.9% in April. Most manufacturing subsectors remained in decline, led by food, beverages and tobacco (-4.5% vs -1.6%), rubber, plastics and non-metallic mineral products (-4.9% vs -4.0%), and electrical equipment (-5.3% vs -1.5%). Meanwhile, output in electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply also fell (-9.6% vs 0.2%), while mining and quarrying production continued to grow, albeit at a slower pace (47.0% vs 72.1%). On a seasonally and working-day adjusted basis, industrial output rose 2.3% in May, rebounding from a 1.1% decline in April.

【三部門釋出通知 規範公立醫療衛生機構醫用裝置集中採購】為規範有序推進醫用裝置集中採購工作開展，提升公立醫療衛生機構資金使用效益，加強醫藥衛生領域廉政建設，保障人民群眾健康權益，國家衛生健康委、國家中醫藥管理局、國家疾病預防控制局今天釋出通知，要求各地進一步做好公立醫療衛生機構醫用裝置集中採購工作。全國公立醫療衛生機構購置列入國家、省、市地級部門集中採購目錄的醫用裝置，均應實施部門集中採購。國家衛生健康委、省級衛生健康行政部門分別制定本級醫用裝置部門集中採購目錄，按規定報同級財政部門備案後組織實施。市地級衛生健康行政部門制定部門集中採購目錄，報省級衛生健康行政部門同意後，再按所在地規定報財政部門備案後實施。甲類大型醫用裝置（主要是高階放射治療類裝置）納入國家級部門集中採購目錄，繼續由國家衛生健康委組織集中採購。個性化定製程度高、需配套設計並建設基礎設施工程、逐臺按程式獲得註冊的裝置（主要是重離子質子放射治療系統），暫不納入集中採購目錄。乙類大型醫用裝置（包括PET/MR、PET/CT、腹腔內窺鏡手術系統和常規放射治療類裝置）納入省級部門集中採購目錄，購置資金投入量大、市場競爭不充分的其他醫用裝置逐步納入集中採購目錄，由省級衛生健康行政部門組織集中採購。國家和省級部門集中採購目錄以外的醫用裝置，由市地級衛生健康行政部門本著穩妥審慎、先易後難的原則，擬定集中採購目錄，組織集中採購。（央視新聞）

### 2026-07-14 14:48:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Swiss Producer and Import Prices Extend Decline — Switzerland’s producer and import prices dropped by 2.1% year-on-year in June 2026, accelerating from a 1.8% fall in the previous month and extending a three-year deflationary streak. The latest reading also marked the steepest annual decline since March, reflecting continued weakness in both domestic and imported prices. Producer prices fell 2.7%, driven by lower prices for manufactured goods, particularly chemical and pharmaceutical products, electricity and gas supply, and computer, electronic and optical products, including watches. Import prices also declined 0.8%, as cheaper petroleum products, petroleum and natural gas, pharmaceutical preparations, and food products outweighed higher prices for basic metals, semi-finished metal products, and computers. On a monthly basis, producer and import prices fell 0.3%, reversing a 0.8% increase in May, mainly due to lower petroleum-related prices.

UK Gas Prices Climb to Over 3-Month High — UK natural gas prices rose toward 128 pence per therm on Tuesday, hitting their highest level in more than three months after President Donald Trump reimposed a US blockade on Iranian ports and announced a 20% security fee on all cargo shipped through the Strait of Hormuz. The blockade, scheduled to take effect at 4:00 p.m. EDT on Tuesday, has dampened expectations for a near-term normalization of shipping activity through the vital waterway. LNG exports from the Middle East had only recently begun to recover, with Qatar ramping up production and shipments following the US-Iran interim agreement in June. However, the renewed hostilities have abruptly halted that recovery, raising concerns over fresh disruptions to LNG exports from the region. Such disruptions could tighten global gas supplies just as Europe is racing to refill storage facilities ahead of next winter.

【小摩：騰訊仍是中國網際網路中最高質量的複利增長股，重申“增持”評級】摩根大通發表報告，重申對騰訊的“增持”評級，目標價維持690港元。該行認為，騰訊仍是中國網際網路中最高質量的複利增長股，擁有持久的使用者參與度、不斷改善的遊戲和廣告變現能力、強勁的利潤率與資產負債表實力，以及具備切實變現路徑的AI選擇權。報告指出，由於騰訊加大了AI投資並預期從今年下半年開始產生折舊，該行將騰訊2026財年的預測非國際財務報告準則(Non-IFRS)每股盈利預測下調5%至28.47元，並將2026年的資本開支預測上調至2000億元。不過，該行強調上述調整反映的是更沉重的AI投資與折舊，而非騰訊既有核心業務的惡化。該行預計第二季收入將同比增長約9%，其中增值服務收入增長5%至6%，遊戲收入增長9%至10%，營銷服務收入增長18%至19%，金融科技收入則增長約5%，與市場預期基本一致。

### 2026-07-14 14:32:35  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #US10Y #Impact mixed #XAUUSD #DXY

【June CPI preview: superficial dip versus rebound risk】June CPI likely reflects a clear statistical lag: headline may drop from May’s 4.2% to roughly 3.8% mainly due to a near-term cooling in energy, not a durable easing of inflation. The Cleveland Fed model projects about 3.9% headline and core CPI around 2.9%, indicating persistent price pressure. Energy weakness could improve nominal inflation, but housing, services and wages continue to support core; the NY Fed survey shows 1- and 3-year inflation expectations rising, so the Fed will need to judge persistence even if June prints softer. Market impact hinges on interpretation—if core remains elevated or energy re-firms in July, traders will reprice second-round inflation risk; amid widening Fed internal divisions and weaker forward guidance, CPI is a live input for the interest-rate path and asset pricing.

【里昂：比亞迪主導地位將回歸，重申“高度確信跑贏大市”評級】里昂發表研報指，比亞迪築底已經完成，預計其主導地位將在今年下半年迴歸，並釋放海外市場和儲能系統(ESS)的增長空間。該里昂指出，比亞迪於今年上半年海外銷量達79.22萬輛，同比增長71%。隨著渠道擴張和新車型推廣，預期下半年海外出口動能將會加速，預計2026年全年銷量將超過180萬輛，2027年將達到約250萬輛的歷史新高，貢獻約80%的利潤。國內市場方面，第二代閃充車型產能預計在下半年逐步釋放，預期將佔2026年總交付量的約30%，且新車型平均溢價達5000元至20000元，足以抵銷當前的成本通脹。此外，該行提及比亞迪在儲能系統(ESS)領域具備領先優勢，其閃充技術生態系統較行業領先6至12個月。  里昂預測比亞迪第二季營業收入將達到2126億元，環比增長42%；淨利潤將升至72億元，環比增長76.7%，主要受惠於強勁的海外產品組合和高階化發展。該行重申其“高度確信跑贏大市”評級，維持H股目標價120港元及A股目標價120元。

【國海證券：維持紫光股份“買入”評級，子公司新華三實現內資全控】國海證券研報指出，紫光股份Q2業績有望同比翻倍增長，子公司新華三實現內資全控。預計上半年歸母淨利潤19.1至23.2億元，同比+83.50%至+122.89%。預計2026Q2單季歸母淨利潤11.22至15.32億元，同比+62.08%至121.31%，環比+42.42%至+94.45%。子公司新華三股權整合，實現內資全控。6月10日，公司全資子公司紫光國際和六家投資者合計受讓新華三19%股權，已完成全部對價支付及股份交割，即惠普所持公司最後一筆股權完成交割。本次交易完成後，新華三正式成為一家純內資企業，公司透過紫光國際持有新華三的股權比例由81%增至87.98%。據IDC釋出的2026年第一季度中國網路市場份額排名，新華三以37%的市場份額穩居中國企業網市場第一。公司積極把握智算和國產化領域帶來的市場機遇，持續深耕行業市場；加快產品研發和技術創新。維持“買入”評級。

【估算顯示初夏熱浪致英格蘭和威爾士超2700人死亡】英國倫敦衛生與熱帶醫學院日前釋出的一項分析估算顯示，5月和6月發生在英格蘭和威爾士的兩次熱浪可能造成超過2700人死亡。這項分析由倫敦衛生與熱帶醫學院、帝國理工學院和英國氣象局等機構研究人員開展。研究人員估算，在英格蘭和威爾士，5月21日至29日的熱浪期間，可能有約550例死亡與高溫相關；6月18日至28日的熱浪期間，可能有約2200例死亡與高溫相關。不過，研究人員強調這項分析結果只是估算而非實際死亡統計。（新華社）

【Japan to stress BOJ independence in economic blueprint】Japan will add a footnote to its new economic and fiscal policy guidelines citing the law that protects the Bank of Japan's independence in setting monetary policy, Tokyo TV reported. The final text will urge the BOJ to aim for a stable rise in prices and to appropriately guide monetary policy, language intended to signal the government does not intend to intervene. An earlier draft sparked yen and JGB selling after markets feared Prime Minister TAKAICHI's administration might pressure the BOJ to delay tightening; cabinet drafters said they would soften the wording to calm market concerns. Iron Ore Rises on China Supply Curbs — Iron ore futures climbed above CNY 750 per ton on Tuesday, reaching a near one-month high as impending Chinese restrictions on selected Fortescue portside cargoes are expected to tighten domestic supply. China Mineral Resources Group, the state-backed commodity buyer, has reportedly informed some steel mills that beginning July 15 they will no longer be allowed to take delivery of Fortescue’s Super Special Fines and Fortune Fines, both lower-grade iron ore products. Separately, China established a new mining investment vehicle, Guangyan International Investment Co., to strengthen its control over overseas mineral resources. Meanwhile, customs data showed China’s iron ore imports rose 15% month-on-month to 112.69 million tons in June. At the same time, disruptions linked to the closure of the Strait of Hormuz have pushed up shipping and raw material input costs, increasing the cost of seaborne iron ore.

Brent futures rallied more than 9% overnight; China’s domestic front‑month crude contract rose above 500 yuan/barrel. Traders cite tightened physical flows and elevated risk as drivers; watch US‑Iran developments — any de‑escalation signals could spur further volatility. The US announced a blockade of Iranian ports effective 04:00 on the 15th and said it would resume a blockade campaign, imposing a 20% levy on all cargo movements. Washington says the measure applies to all vessels regardless of flag and covers Iran’s entire coastline, including ports and oil terminals, while allowing neutral ships to transit the Strait of Hormuz to or from non‑Iranian destinations; humanitarian shipments are permitted subject to inspection. Shipping data show tanker transits through Hormuz fell to a two‑month low in the past 24 hours. Separately, Iran’s oil sales remain slow and independent refiners are reportedly switching to cheaper Iraqi, UAE and Qatari crude, reinforcing upward pressure on prices amid rising conflict intensity.

### 2026-07-14 14:12:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Wholesale Prices YoY #Importance 2

Germany Wholesale Prices Rise 4.9% in June — Germany's wholesale prices rose 4.9% year-on-year in June 2026, easing from a 5.9% increase in May. Despite the slowdown, the latest reading marked the 19th consecutive month of wholesale price inflation, with energy products and raw materials remaining the main drivers amid the conflict in the Middle East. The sharpest price increases were recorded for mineral oil products (21.7%) and non-ferrous ores, non-ferrous metals, and semi-finished non-ferrous metal products (31.0%), followed by chemical products (14.9%) and iron, steel, and semi-finished ferrous metal products (8.2%). These increases, however, were partly offset by lower prices for live animals (-16.0%), coffee, tea, cocoa, and spices (-10.0%), and milk, dairy products, eggs, edible fats, and oils (-8.5%). On a monthly basis, wholesale prices fell 0.7% in June, following a 0.6% decline in May, marking the second consecutive monthly decline and contrasted with market expectations for a 0.5% rise.

Rupiah Strengthens After S&P Affirms Indonesia's Credit Rating — The Indonesian rupiah firmed below IDR 18,100 per U.S. dollar on Tuesday, recovering from losses in the prior two sessions, after S&P Global Ratings reaffirmed Indonesia’s investment-grade credit rating with a stable outlook. The agency said fiscal and external strains, driven by higher oil prices and rupiah weakness, are likely temporary. Bank Indonesia also lent support, having raised rates by 100 bps between May and June and vowing to use all tools to steady the currency. Fiscal prospects improved as the House Budget Committee proposed trimming the 2027 budget for President Prabowo’s free meal program to boost efficiency. Still, gains were capped by signs of softer domestic momentum, with oil prices staying elevated, consumer sentiment falling for a third month in June, and retail sales declining the most in three years. On the global front, the U.S. dollar held firm ahead of key inflation data and Fed Chair Kevin Warsh’s first semiannual testimony before Congress.

### 2026-07-14 14:06:34  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Gains on Indonesia Supply Controls — Nickel traded around $16,830 per tonne, climbing from six-month low levels near $16,300, supported by easing supply concerns. Indonesia reaffirmed it would not approve a broad expansion of nickel production quotas, with additional approvals limited to smelters facing raw material shortages. The policy reinforced expectations of relatively tight ore availability, as the government seeks to prevent oversupply and stabilize the global nickel market while allowing only targeted production increases. At the same time, renewed Middle East tensions kept oil prices elevated and sustained concerns over sulfur supply, a key input for Indonesia's nickel processing industry, providing additional support to the market. However, gains were capped by subdued physical demand, with high-grade nickel pig iron trading remaining quiet, downstream restocking muted, and nickel ore prices continuing to soften amid ample inventories and persistent supply availability.

NZX 50 Extends Losses, Down 0.5% at Close — The NZX 50 dropped 72 points, or 0.5%, to 13,651 on Tuesday, falling for the second consecutive session and tracking the decline on Wall Street overnight after US President Trump vowed to reinstate a blockade on Iranian shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. Rising oil prices due to the escalating conflict in the Middle East continued to weigh on sentiment as inflation concerns fueled expectations of further interest rate hikes. Sentiment was also pressured by a warning from RBNZ Chief Economist Paul Conway that sticky inflation caused by recent Middle East supply shocks could lead to further interest rate hikes. On the data front, New Zealand's visitor arrivals rose 6.7% in May, marking the slowest growth in four months. The financial, industrials, and utilities sectors led the declines, with notable losses from Auckland International Airport (-3.3%), ANZ Group (-2.5%), Infratil (-1.9%), Seeka Limited (-1.0%), Summerset Group (-0.9%), and Westpac Banking Corp (-0.8%).

The phased introduction of next-generation high-bandwidth memory HBM4 should sustain upward pressure on HBM prices. ### 2026-07-14 13:20:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Finnish Inflation Rate Unchanged in June — The annual inflation rate in Finland stood at 2.1% in June 2026, unchanged from the previous month. It remained the highest reading since March 2024, as prices increased at a faster pace for housing and utilities (1.9% vs 0.6% in May) and restaurants and accommodation (2.1% vs 1.8%) while deflation softened for insurance and financial services (-1.6% vs -2.6%) and personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (-1.8% vs -2.2%). Meanwhile, costs particularly slowed for food and non-alcoholic beverages (0.7% vs 1.1%), transport (6.2% vs 7.1%), furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (3.5% vs 4.3%), health (4% vs 6.2%), and information and communication (1.5% vs 4.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 0.2%, reversing a 0.5% gain in May and marking the first monthly decline since January. Meanwhile, harmonized consumer prices, the ECB's preferred measure, rose 2.7% year-on-year in June, following a 2.8% rise in the prior month.
